<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子热化与局域化之间的边界是多体系统和量子信息科学的核心前沿问题，本文旨在探究具有长程三体相互作用的非局域三波混频模型中的热化与局域化行为。

Method: 采用精确对角化技术，分析全局能级间距统计、动态可观测量、特征态反参与比、希尔伯特空间碎片化、离时序关联子（OTOC）、特征态纠缠熵涨落等。

Result: 发现全局能级统计显示可积性，但动态可观测量和特征态反参与比表明遍历性和去局域化；系统存在强希尔伯特空间碎片化；碎片化扇区内动力学快速混乱，全局输运势垒导致对数弛豫；晚时OTOC平均值随系统尺寸缩放。

Conclusion: 揭示了运动学约束而非显式全局对称性驱动的强希尔伯特空间碎片化是上述矛盾行为的原因，晚时OTOC平均值的系统尺寸缩放可作为三体动力学势垒的可实验观测特征。

Abstract: Understanding the boundaries between quantum thermalization and localization in many-body systems remains a central frontier of condensed matter and quantum information science. In this work, we investigate the dynamics and spectral properties of a generic model with long-range three-body-interaction, namely, a system with non-local three-wave-mixing. This model has been realized recently with a microwave Fabry-Perot cavity terminated on one end by a superconducting qubit mirror. Utilizing exact diagonalization techniques, we uncover a striking paradox: the global energy level spacing statistics show integrability, even though all dynamic observables and inverse participation ratios of the eigenstates indicate ergodicity and delocalization. We show that this behavior is a hallmark of strong Hilbert space fragmentation driven by kinematic constraints rather than an explicit global symmetry. Inside these sectors, dynamics scramble rapidly, as evidenced by the out-of-time-ordered correlator (OTOC), while global transport is heavily bottlenecked, resulting in a logarithmic relaxation to equilibrium. This picture is further confirmed by fluctuations in eigenstate entanglement entropy at the same energy. Finally, we demonstrate that the late time OTOC average scales with system size, providing a distinct experimentally accessible signature of the underlying three-body kinetic bottlenecks.

</details>


### [99] [Quasi-polar Decomposition of Quantum Neural Networks via Adaptive Non-local Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27051)
*Shih-Hao Ho,Yan Li,Huan-Hsin Tseng,Hsin-Yi Lin,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Shinjae Yoo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出DANO（对角自适应非局部可观测量）作为变分量子电路模型演化的规范分解方法，将学习到的可观测量分解为对角谱和酉基，使训练过程转化为谱空间和李代数空间中的轨迹。实验表明，DANO径向谱扩展与准确率相关，角度坐标揭示了主导的准确率相关分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变分量子电路模型的训练过程缺乏有效的规范分解方法来表征其演化特性，需要一种能够从谱空间和李代数空间角度理解量子模型行为的新框架。

Method: 采用DANO将每个学习到的可观测量分解为对角谱（径向坐标）和酉基（通过李群识别的角度坐标），形成准极坐标描述，将训练过程视为谱空间和李代数空间中的轨迹。

Result: 在两个分类任务上，DANO径向谱扩展与模型准确率呈现相关性，且角度坐标中存在一个主导的准确率相关分量。

Conclusion: DANO框架为表征量子模型行为提供了新的视角，通过谱-李代数空间分解能够捕捉与模型性能相关的关键特征。

Abstract: We use Diagonal Adaptive Non-local Observables (DANO) as a canonical decomposition for studying Variational Quantum Circuit model evolution. Separating each learned observable into a diagonal spectrum and a unitary basis gives a quasi-polar description: the spectral weights are viewed as radial coordinates, while the unitary circuit serves as angular coordinates through Lie group identifications. This turns the training process into a trajectory in spectral and Lie-algebra space.
  Experiments on two classification tasks show that DANO radial spectral expansion correlates with accuracy. DANO angle coordinates reveal a dominant accuracy-correlated component. The framework provides a different perspective to characterize quantum model behavior.

</details>


### [100] [Optimising Trotter-Suzuki Simulations of Markovian Open Quantum Systems via Classical Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27060)
*S. M. Pillay,I. J. David,I. Sinayskiy,F. Petruccione*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对开放量子系统在数字量子计算机上的Trotter-Suzuki乘积公式模拟，推导了确定性与随机化一阶/二阶方法的解析界限，并提出了基于Liouvillian项的diamond范数估计与二分搜索的高效经典算法来优化Trotter步数。在XX自旋链和横场伊辛模型上的数值结果表明，解析界限过于保守，而经验优化界限可显著降低资源需求，其中二阶随机化TS-PF在大型系统中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确估计Trotter步数（进而门复杂度）对于最小化开放量子系统数字化量子模拟的计算成本至关重要，而现有理论界限往往过于保守，需要更精准的资源估计方法。

Method: 1) 推导一阶/二阶确定性和随机化TS-PF的解析界限，将Trotter步数与模型参数、演化时间和精度直接关联；2) 提出一种经典算法，利用单个Liouvillian项的diamond范数估计和二分搜索来优化Trotter步数。

Result: 在XX自旋链（边界驱动+局域退相干）和横场伊辛模型上，解析界限过于保守，而经验优化界限可显著减少Trotter步数；二阶随机化TS-PF在大型系统中资源需求最低。

Conclusion: 经验界限策略对于实现马尔可夫开放量子系统的高效资源模拟具有重要意义，二阶随机化TS-PF是资源最优的方案。

Abstract: Simulating an open quantum system on a digital quantum computer often involves the use of Trotter-Suzuki (TS) Product Formulas (PF) to approximate the system's time evolution. Precise estimates for the required number of Trotter steps (and hence the overall gate count) can be crucial for minimising the computational cost of these methods. Building on established theoretical guarantees, we derive analytic bounds for the First- and Second-Order Deterministic and Randomised TS-PF, directly relating the number of Trotter steps to the model parameters, evolution time and precision. These bounds enable concrete resource estimation for each method. We then present a computationally efficient classical algorithm that uses diamond norm estimates of individual Liouvillian terms and a binary search to significantly reduce the Trotter steps required for a target precision. Our numerical results on two prototypical models - an XX-Spin Chain with boundary driving and local dephasing, and a Transverse-Field Ising Model - show that the theoretical (analytic) bounds are often overly conservative, whereas the empirical (optimised) bounds yield a significantly smaller number of Trotter steps for the same precision. Among the methods investigated, the Second-Order Randomised TS-PF typically achieves the lowest resource demands, especially for larger systems. These findings emphasise the significance of empirical bounding strategies in achieving more resource-efficient simulations of Markovian open quantum systems.

</details>


### [101] [Adaptive Multi-Backend Simulation of Near-Clifford Quantum Circuits via Spatial Stabilizer-Frame Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27075)
*Héctor J. García*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合Feynman路径求和与稳定子框架仿真的Clifford+T量子电路精确振幅模拟器，通过递归二分、自动回退和成本模型驱动分区选择，在结构化基准上相比单体稳定子框架仿真加速92-17645倍，相比生产级状态向量仿真器加速最高47.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于稳定子的Schrödinger-Feynman方法存在三个局限：缺乏递归多级二分、没有内存超限时的自动回退、以及依赖不可靠的割计数最小化启发式。实验表明割计数最小化在实践中不可靠——全局更干净的分区可能因T门密度不平衡而增加实际运行时间。

Method: 在平衡量子比特二分上结合Feynman路径求和与稳定子框架仿真，并做了三个方向扩展：(1)递归多级二分构建二叉树；(2)叶节点稳定子帧超内存上限时自动回退到稠密态向量仿真；(3)用成本模型替代割计数最小化启发式，该模型用2^w代替2^n作为每侧上限，显式建模每振幅读出成本，并发现并修复了叶仿真器中路径末端振幅提取的二次渐近低效问题（替换为O(F*s*n)单振幅内积）。

Result: 在结构化n=16分层基准上，递归仿真器相比单体稳定子框架仿真加速92-17645倍，相同割下每路径比稠密半态向量基线快79倍，端到端比生产级态向量仿真器快最高47.9倍（中位数约5倍）。在对抗性随机Clifford+T电路上，稠密态向量在交叉割门数超过n/2时胜出——与成本模型预测一致。

Conclusion: 跨割Feynman求和是主要开销，但具有尴尬并行特性（常数量通信开销），优于近期矩阵乘积态-稳定子张量方法的顺序内收缩循环。

Abstract: We present an exact amplitude simulator for Clifford+T quantum circuits that combines a Feynman path sum across a balanced qubit bipartition with stabilizer-frame simulation on each half. The construction extends prior stabilizer-based Schrödinger-Feynman methods in three directions: recursive multilevel bipartition into a binary tree, automatic fallback to dense state-vector simulation when a leaf's stabilizer frame would exceed its memory ceiling, and a cost-model-driven partition selector that replaces the standard cut-count minimization heuristic.
  We show cut-count minimization is an unreliable proxy in practice: a globally cleaner partition can reduce cross-cut count yet increase wall-clock time, because it imbalances T-gate density across halves and inflates per-half stabilizer-frame size. Our cost model substitutes the stabilizer-frame bound 2w for the dense 2n ceiling per side and explicitly models per-amplitude readout cost; isolating that term uncovered a quadratic-asymptotic inefficiency in the leaf simulator's end-of-path amplitude extraction, fixed by replacing it with an existing O(F * s * n) single-amplitude inner product.
  On a structured hierarchical n=16 benchmark the recursive simulator beats monolithic stabilizer-frame simulation by 92x to 17,645x, wins by 79x per path against a dense half-state-vector baseline under an identical cut, and beats a production state-vector simulator end to end by up to 47.9x (median ~5x). On adversarial random Clifford+T circuits the dense state vector wins past a crossover near n/2 cross-cut gates -- the regime the cost model identifies. The dominant cost, the cross-cut Feynman sum, is embarrassingly parallel with constant inter-worker communication, unlike recent matrix-product-state stabilizer-tensor methods whose inner contraction loop is sequential.

</details>


### [102] [Native CCZ Gate with Fluxonium Qubits and a Microwave-Driven Coupler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27094)
*Grigoriy S. Mazhorin,Tatyana A. Chudakova,Alena S. Kazmina,Nikolai G. Berezkin,Arina V. Zotova,Artyom M. Polyanskiy,Nikolay N. Abramov,Mikhail A. Tarkhov,Alexander M. Mumlyakov,Igor V. Trofimov,Elizaveta A. Krivko,Nikita Yu. Rudenko,Maxim V. Chichkov,Vladimir I. Chichkov,Ilya A. Simakov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在超导磁通量子比特处理器中，通过微波驱动transmon耦合器，实验实现了65纳秒的原生受控-受控-相位门（等效于Toffoli门），保真度达99.39(5)%，仅需单次控制脉冲和简单校准，且可扩展至二维布局，证明了原生多量子比特门作为可扩展超导量子处理器高效原语的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生多量子比特门有望减少分解为单比特和两比特操作所带来的开销，但其能否在可扩展架构中同时实现高保真度、简单控制以及对寄生相互作用的鲁棒性，尚不清楚。本文旨在通过实验验证这一可行性。

Method: 基于磁通量子比特（fluxonium）通过微波驱动transmon耦合器构成的三量子比特处理器单元，实现65纳秒的原生受控-受控-相位门（CCZ门，局部等价于Toffoli门）。采用单控制脉冲实现，校准过程简单，性能受限于相干时间。

Result: CCZ门保真度达99.39(5)%，若通过传统分解实现则要求CZ门保真度约99.94%。该方案自然可扩展至二维布局且寄生相互作用极低。

Conclusion: 原生多量子比特门是一种硬件高效的原语，适用于可扩展超导量子处理器。

Abstract: Native multi-qubit gates could reduce the overhead associated with decompositions into single- and two-qubit operations, but whether they can simultaneously provide high fidelity, simple control and robustness against parasitic interactions in scalable architectures remains unclear. Here we experimentally realize a 65-ns native controlled-controlled-phase operation, locally equivalent to the Toffoli gate, with a fidelity of 99.39(5)% in a three-qubit processor unit based on fluxonium qubits coupled via a microwave-driven transmon coupler. The implemented operation would require CZ fidelities of approximately 99.94% if realized through a conventional decomposition. The gate is implemented with a single control pulse, that relies on a simple calibration procedure yielding coherence-limited performance. This processor unit naturally extends to scalable two-dimensional layouts with low parasitic interactions. Altogether, these results establish native multi-qubit gates as a viable hardware-efficient primitive for scalable superconducting quantum processors.

</details>


### [103] [Effective Hamiltonians for Predictive Quantum Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27111)
*Razvan Stanescu,Hugo Ribeiro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超导transmon量子比特中，基于Duffing近似模型和对角化transmon模型推导出的单量子比特门控制脉冲的差异。发现Duffing模型导出的修正场在该模型内能有效降低门误差，但转移到对角化transmon模型时效果不佳，在快速门区域甚至无法改善基线。模型依赖的能谱和驱动算子表示的微小差异会在驱动演化中累积，导致不同的误差生成器和修正脉冲。AC Stark相位积累的失配是模型依赖性的一个诊断指标。忽略相关泄漏通道或高阶误差通道会导致过度受限的修正策略，而包含这些通道可扩展修正框架，利用相同物理资源提升门性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子控制的高保真度依赖于精确的驱动动力学模型，但不同理论模型（如Duffing近似与对角化transmon模型）对同一物理系统的描述存在差异，这种模型依赖性如何影响控制脉冲的设计和门保真度尚不清楚。本文旨在系统研究这种模型差异对单量子比特门控制性能的影响。

Method: 采用相同的修正脉冲构建方法，对比Duffing近似模型和对角化transmon模型预测的控制脉冲；通过分析能谱差异、驱动算子表示差异、AC Stark相位积累以及误差生成器，诊断模型依赖性的来源；并引入包含泄漏通道和高阶误差通道的扩展修正框架。

Result: Duffing模型的修正场在该模型内能有效降低门误差，但转移到对角化transmon模型时效果显著下降，快速门区域甚至无法改善基线。模型间在能谱和驱动算子表示上的微小差异会在驱动演化中累积放大，导致不同的误差生成器和最优修正脉冲。AC Stark相位失配是模型依赖性的一个关键诊断指标。忽略相关泄漏或高阶误差通道会限制修正策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 量子控制脉冲的设计高度依赖于所选理论模型，模型间不可忽略的动力学差异意味着从一个模型导出的修正不能直接迁移到另一个模型。设计高保真度控制时须考虑模型的能谱准确性、驱动算子表示以及高阶误差通道，扩展修正框架可更有效地利用相同物理资源提升门性能。

Abstract: High-fidelity quantum control relies on accurate models of driven dynamics. We examine this re- quirement for single-qubit gates in superconducting transmons by comparing control pulses derived from the standard Duffing approximation and from a Hamiltonian constructed by diagonalizing the transmon eigenbasis. Using the same correction-pulse construction for both models, we show that correction fields derived from the Duffing approximation can substantially reduce the gate error pre- dicted by that model while remaining less effective when combined with an independently calibrated baseline pulse in the diagonalized-transmon model. In the fast-gate regime, such transferred correc- tions can even fail to improve over the uncorrected diagonalized-transmon baseline. We show that small model-dependent differences in both the energy spectrum and the representation of the drive operator can compound during driven evolution, resulting in different predicted error generators and correction pulses. A mismatch in the accumulated AC Stark phase provides one illustrative di- agnostic of this dynamical model dependence. We further demonstrate that the model Hamiltonian informs the choice of control framework: Omitting relevant leakage pathways or higher-order error channels can lead to an overly restricted correction strategy. Including these channels motivates an extended correction framework that improves the gate performance using the same physical control resources.

</details>


### [104] [Ky Fan majorization for binary tensor products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27116)
*Michael M. Wolf,Yuanheng Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了二元张量积矩阵之和的奇异值的一个Ky Fan型优超关系的简短证明，将Alhejji的结果从两项和推广到任意和，从正矩阵推广到任意矩阵，并应用于完全正映射与其Kraus算子的奇异值之间的优超关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结果仅适用于两项和及正矩阵，需要将其推广到更一般的情形。

Method: 通过简短证明，将Ky Fan型优超关系从两项和推广到任意数量的二元张量积之和，并将适用矩阵从正矩阵扩展到任意矩阵。

Result: 得到了关于二元张量积矩阵之和的奇异值的一个Ky Fan型优超关系，并建立了完全正映射与其Kraus算子的奇异值之间的优超关系。

Conclusion: 该推广统一并扩展了已有的优超关系，为量子信息等领域提供了更普适的工具。

Abstract: We provide a short proof of a Ky Fan-type majorization relation for the singular values of a sum of binary tensor products of matrices. This generalizes Alhejji's result (arXiv:2410.18254) from two summands to arbitrary sums and from positive matrices to arbitrary matrices. As an application, we show a majorization relation between the singular values of a completely positive map and those of its Kraus operators.

</details>


### [105] [The Keyl-Werner algorithm is not optimal for spectrum estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27117)
*Angelos Pelecanos,Jack Spilecki,Ewin Tang,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子态谱估计算法，仅需 n = O(d²·(log log d / log d)²) 份副本即可将特征值估计到常数误差（全变差距离），优于 Keyl-Werner 算法的 Θ(d²) 样本复杂度，解决了 Keyl 和 Werner 在 2001 年提出的开放问题，并反驳了 Wright 在 2016 年的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全量子态层析需要 Θ(d²) 份副本，而谱估计（特征值学习）能否用更少的样本完成是一个长期未决的问题。Keyl 和 Werner 在 2001 年提出该问题，Wright 在 2016 年猜测 Θ(d²) 是必要的。本文试图突破这一下界。

Method: 核心技术是一种新的量子态层析保证：对于所有方向 |w⟩，层析误差与 ⟨w|ρ|w⟩ 成正比（即相对误差界）。从这一更强的相对误差保证出发，推导出谱估计的改进样本复杂度。

Result: 实现了 n = O(d²·(log log d / log d)²) 的样本复杂度，显著优于 Θ(d²) 的已知下界。同时，作为推论，在 Bures 距离下的主成分分析和 χ²-散度下的层析也获得了改进。

Conclusion: 谱估计可以比全态层析使用更少的副本，解决了二十多年的开放问题，并展示了相对误差层析作为通用工具的价值。

Abstract: We give an algorithm which, given $n = O(d^2 \cdot (\log\log(d)/\log(d))^2)$ copies of $ρ$, estimates the eigenvalues of $ρ$ to constant error in total variation distance. Thus, we can learn the eigenvalues of a quantum state with fewer copies than the $Θ(d^2)$ needed to run full state tomography. This is the first improvement to spectrum estimation over the influential Keyl-Werner algorithm, which uses $n = Θ(d^2)$ copies, thereby resolving a question raised by Keyl and Werner in 2001 and refuting a 2016 conjecture of Wright.
  Our main technical tool is a new tomography guarantee, where the error of tomography in a particular direction $|w\rangle$ scales with $\langle w | ρ|w\rangle$ for all directions simultaneously. From this stronger "relative-error" bound, we recover better algorithms for principal component analysis in Bures distance and tomography in $χ^2$-divergence as corollaries.

</details>


### [106] [Hardware-efficient erasure-error detection with an integer fluxonium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27123)
*Junyoung An,Helin Zhang,Jeffrey M. Gertler,Kate Azar,Renée DePencier Piñero,Michael Gingras,Junghyun Kim,Bethany M. Niedzielski,Ilan T. Rosen,Mollie E. Schwartz,Joel Î-j. Wang,Terry P. Orlando,Jeffrey A. Grover,Max Hays,Kyle Serniak,William D. Oliver*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在整数磁通量子里证明了擦除转换和电路中擦除检测，通过将|g⟩,|f⟩作为逻辑态、|e⟩作为擦除态，实现了8.4倍的|f⟩态寿命提升、1.38倍的Hahn-echo时间提升以及单量子比特门错误率从0.061%降至0.030%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过擦除错误检测提高量子纠错效率，揭示错误事件的时间和位置。

Method: 在整数磁通量子里将|g⟩,|f⟩编码为逻辑态、|e⟩编码为擦除态；抑制|f⟩→|g⟩直接跃迁，将主导的|f⟩→|e⟩跃迁转换为可检测擦除；通过设计空间消除逻辑态间谐振频率偏移，实现无需辅助比特的电路中擦除检测。

Result: 丢弃检测到的擦除事件后，|f⟩态寿命提升8.4倍，Hahn-echo时间提升1.38倍，单比特门错误率从0.061(2)%降至0.030(5)%。

Conclusion: 整数磁通量子是用于擦除错误检测和转换的高效硬件平台，但仍需改进以实现具有高擦除偏置的有效擦除量子比特。

Abstract: Erasure-error detection can improve the efficiency of quantum error correction by revealing the times and locations of their error events. In this work, we demonstrate erasure conversions and mid-circuit erasure detections in a single integer fluxonium, in which the states $\mathrm{|g\rangle, |f\rangle}$ encode the logical states and $\mathrm{|e\rangle}$ encodes the erasure state. The integer fluxonium suppresses direct $|\mathrm{f} \rangle \rightarrow |\mathrm{g} \rangle$ transitions and allows the dominant $|\mathrm{f} \rangle \rightarrow |\mathrm{e}\rangle$ transitions to be converted into detectable erasures. Furthermore, we identified a design space that nullifies the resonant-frequency shift between the two logical states, enabling ancilla-free mid-circuit erasure checks using the same resonator employed for final readout. By discarding the detected erasure events, we achieved an 8.4-fold increase in the $|\mathrm{f}\rangle$ state lifetime, a 1.38-fold increase in the Hahn-echo time, and a reduction of single-qubit gate error from 0.061(2)% to 0.030(5)%. Our results establish integer fluxonium as a hardware-efficient platform for erasure-error detection and conversion, while identifying the improvements required to realize an effective erasure qubit with high erasure bias.

</details>


### [107] [Parity-Based Time-Bin Encoding Enabling SWAP Between Polarization and Time-Bin Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27144)
*Adam Sultan,Connor Kupchak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于奇偶性的时间仓编码方案，使物理延迟能够实现逻辑时间仓翻转，进而通过三个CNOT门组合实现偏振与时间仓自由度之间的确定性SWAP操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统早/晚时间仓编码不支持从晚到早的双向逻辑时间仓翻转，限制了某些量子操作的实现。为了解决这一问题，需要一种新的编码方式使时间仓翻转变得双向可行。

Method: 提出奇偶性时间仓编码：逻辑|0⟩_T和|1⟩_T对应Δt的偶数倍和奇数倍间隔，使Δt的物理延迟实现|0⟩_T↔|1⟩_T。利用这种编码，偏振控制延迟线实现CNOT_{P→T}，周期性折射率调制实现CNOT_{T→P}，三个CNOT组合成确定性SWAP。

Result: 分析了基于场的调制偏振旋转误差概率，以及由EOM驱动电子学和光子探测共同决定的时序分辨率约束。

Conclusion: 奇偶性时间仓编码是实现偏振与时间仓自由度之间确定性SWAP的关键使能要素，为多自由度光子量子处理提供了重要的架构原语。

Abstract: Multi-degree-of-freedom photonic quantum processing requires routing between degree-of-freedom (DOF) qubit encodings on a single photon. A SWAP between polarization and time-bin qubits is Multi-degree-of-freedom photonic quantum processing requires routing between degree-of-freedom (DOF) qubit encodings on a single photon. A SWAP between polarization and time-bin qubits is an advantageous primitive for such architectures, however conventional early/late time-bin encoding does not support bidirectional logical time-bin flips from late to early which limits the ability to implement certain quantum operations. We introduce a parity-based time-bin encoding in which logical $\vert 0 \rangle_T$ and $\vert 1 \rangle_T$ correspond to even and odd multiples of a spacing $Δt$, so that a physical delay of $Δt$ implements $\vert 0 \rangle_T \leftrightarrow \vert 1 \rangle_T$. This encoding is the enabling ingredient that makes a polarization-controlled delay line implement $\mathrm{CNOT}_{P \rightarrow T}$ and aligns naturally with periodic refractive index modulation for $\mathrm{CNOT}_{T \rightarrow P}$. Composing three such CNOT operations sequentially results in a deterministic SWAP between polarization and time-bin degrees of freedom. We analyze field-based modulation polarization-rotation error probability and timing-resolution constraints set by both EOM drive electronics and photon detection.

</details>


### [108] [Improved Methods for Determining Quantum Error Correcting Code Performance and Fault Tolerance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27153)
*Michael Mullan,Matthew Weippert,Winton Brown*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子纠错中低错误率区域性能难以直接模拟的问题，提出了两种改进方法：一种剪枝算法用于去除易纠正的错误并聚焦于恶性核心，以及一种子区域MCMC方法，通过参数化每次重采样错误模式的比例来加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错在大规模量子计算中至关重要，但低错误率下的码性能无法通过直接蒙特卡洛模拟获得，需要从高错误率外推。然而，外推的准确性依赖于不可纠正错误模式的最小权重，而该权重受实现细节影响常未知。MCMC方法虽能模拟低错误率下的逻辑失效率，但现有Metropolis算法收敛缓慢。

Method: 提出两种方法：1) 剪枝算法，去除典型错误模式中大量易纠正的错误，聚焦于低权重恶性核心；2) 子区域MCMC，一种新的Metropolis-Hastings算法族，通过参数控制每次重采样错误模式的比例，在蒙特卡洛和单步MCMC之间插值。

Result: 通过合理选择子区域MCMC的参数，收敛速度远快于先前工作。

Conclusion: 本文提出的剪枝和子区域MCMC方法能有效评估量子纠错码在低错误率下的性能，显著提升收敛速度，为实用化量子计算中的错误分析提供了更高效的模拟工具。

Abstract: One of the central challenges in quantum error correction is determining the performance of a code in the low-error regimes needed to implement utility-scale computations. While performance at these error rates is not amenable to direct Monte Carlo simulation, it can be extrapolated from simulations at higher logical error rates, assuming the logical error rate scales predictably with increasing distance or decreasing physical error rate. However, the expected scaling depends sensitively on the minimum weight of uncorrectable error patterns. In many cases, the minimum weight is unknown since it depends not only on the theoretical code distance, but also on details of the implementation. Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods, as adapted to quantum error correction by Bravyi and Vargo, provide a way to estimate logical failure rates in these low-error regimes via simulation. While offering significant gains over Monte Carlo, the described Metropolis algorithm makes small changes to the current logical failure patterns which results in slow convergence. In this paper, we argue that typical failure patterns include a large number of easily correctable errors that coexist alongside a malignant core. This observation motivates two new approaches to better evaluate code performance. First, we describe a pruning algorithm designed to obviate these correctable errors and focus on the problematic low-weight core. Second, we develop a novel family of Metropolis-Hastings algorithms, referred to as subregion MCMC. This technique is parameterized by the fraction of the error pattern that is resampled at each step, effectively interpolating between Monte Carlo and single step MCMC. We show that a judicious choice of this parameter results in far faster convergence than prior work.

</details>


### [109] [Training Quantum Dragons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27168)
*Muhammad Yusf,Bhola Devkota,Mark A. Novotny,Gautam Rupak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了非平衡格林函数（NEGF）方法的量子计算机实现，通过将NEGF散射问题重构为线性方程组，利用HHL和VQLS算法计算量子龙纳米器件的透射系数，并在IBM量子处理器上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NEGF是纳米尺度电子输运的标准形式，但其经典计算在处理大体系时面临计算复杂度挑战。量子计算有望提供指数级加速，因此需要探索NEGF的量子计算实现。

Method: 将NEGF散射问题重构为线性方程组，其解编码透射和反射振幅；应用HHL和VQLS两种量子线性求解算法；采用相似变换块对角化NEGF线性系统以减少Pauli分解；在单带紧束缚模型下研究量子龙纳米器件。

Result: 2-site和6-site量子龙器件分别映射到3个和4个物理量子比特的紧凑电路；通过理想模拟、含噪声模拟以及在物理IBM量子处理器上的计算，验证了量子计算NEGF的可行性。

Conclusion: 研究表明量子计算机可以成功执行NEGF计算，为未来大规模量子模拟纳米电子输运奠定了基础。

Abstract: The Non-Equilibrium Green's Function (NEGF) is the standard formalism for nano-scale electron transport. By recasting the NEGF scattering problem as a linear system of equations whose solution encodes the transmission and reflection amplitudes, we present the first quantum computerized implementation of NEGF. We apply both the Harrow--Hassidim--Lloyd and Variational Quantum Linear Solver algorithms to compute the transmission coefficient $T(E)$ of quantum dragon nanodevices within the single-band tight-binding model. Quantum dragon devices exhibit perfect transmission across the full conducting band regardless of internal disorder. The problem maps onto compact circuits of 3 and 4 total physical qubits for the 2-site and 6-site dragon devices, respectively. A similarity transformation block-diagonalizes the NEGF linear system reducing the Pauli decomposition of the block-encoded matrix. We demonstrate the feasibility of quantum computation by performing ideal and noise-aware simulations and computations on physical IBM quantum processor.

</details>


### [110] [OQRAM: Oblivious Quantum Random Access Memory for Securing Delegated Quantum Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27171)
*Shifan Xu,Yizhuo Tan,Yongshan Ding,Jakub Szefer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了 oblivious QRAM（ oblivious Quantum Random Access Memory），一种用于隐私保护的委托式量子查询的密码学抽象。协议包含离线刷新和在线保护查询两个阶段，采用加密加打乱的数据库布局，通过基于量子安全伪随机排列（qPRP）或量子一次一密（qOTP）的方法实现相干地址掩码，支持多查询使用，并引入诱饵检查抵御恶意服务器，实现了指数级量子通信缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在委托设置下，标准QRAM查询接口可能向服务器暴露敏感信息，现有全盲量子计算方法量子资源需求过高，需要一种更轻量的隐私保护方案。

Method: 提出oblivious QRAM协议，包含离线刷新和在线保护查询两个阶段。数据库以加密加打乱方式存储，查询时通过qPRP或qOTP进行相干地址掩码保护。qPRP变体支持多查询场景，通过跨查询分发数据库刷新减少经典通信；引入诱饵检查机制抵御恶意服务器。

Result: 在线保护仅增加适度的量子开销（超出查询寄存器部分），避免了本地QRAM构造所需的指数级量子资源。相比全盲量子计算，该框架显著降低了客户端和服务端的量子资源需求，实现了指数级量子通信缩减。

Conclusion: oblivious QRAM提供了一种专用于隐私保护委托式QRAM访问的轻量级抽象，在保证隐私的同时大幅降低了量子资源消耗。

Abstract: Quantum query is a basic subroutine in many quantum algorithms, and Quantum Random Access Memory (QRAM) provides a natural way to realize such coherent query access. In delegated settings, however, a standard QRAM query interface can expose sensitive information to the server. This paper introduces oblivious QRAM, a cryptographic abstraction for privacy-preserving delegated coherent query access. The protocol consists of an offline refresh phase and an online protected query phase. The database is stored in an encrypted and shuffled layout, and each query is protected by coherent address masking using either a quantum-secure pseudorandom permutation (qPRP) based method or a quantum one-time pad (qOTP) based method. In the adopted client model, the online protection adds only modest quantum overhead beyond the query register, avoiding the exponential quantum resources that would otherwise be required by an equivalent local QRAM construction. The qPRP-based variant also supports multi-query use by distributing database refresh across multiple queries to reduce classical communication. To address malicious servers, decoy checks are further incorporated to strengthen privacy protection and enable probabilistic tampering detection. Compared with fully blind quantum computing, this framework provides a lighter abstraction tailored to private delegated QRAM access, significantly reducing quantum resource requirements on both the client and server sides and achieving an exponential reduction in quantum communication.

</details>


### [111] [Classical and Quantum MacWilliams Transforms as Spin Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27173)
*Eric Kubischta,Ian Teixeira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从自旋对称性视角统一推导了经典与量子码中MacWilliams变换的Wigner-D旋转本质，揭示了其仅依赖于平凡与非平凡错误的划分，且长度变化对应自旋量子数变化，旋转轴变化对应不同编码理论间的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MacWilliams变换在经典与量子编码理论中呈现多种形式，缺乏统一的底层解释。本文旨在揭示自旋作为这些变换背后的统一引擎，从运动学角度重新推导所有MacWilliams变换。

Method: 将错误分为平凡与非平凡两类，基于此分裂从运动学角度将MacWilliams变换推导为Wigner-D旋转，即两个规范基之间的转动。通过保持旋转不变、改变长度n（对应自旋n/2）以及固定n、改变旋转轴来统一不同的编码理论。

Result: MacWilliams变换本质上是一个Wigner-D旋转，与具体的编码理论无关；长度变化仅改变自旋表示而不改变旋转本身；旋转轴的选择决定了是经典还是量子编码理论。

Conclusion: 自旋对称性为所有MacWilliams变换提供了统一的运动学解释框架，经典与量子编码理论间的差异仅源于同一自旋旋转的不同投影轴选择。

Abstract: Spin is the hidden engine behind the zoo of MacWilliams transforms in weight enumerator theories - not only for qubits and qudits, but even for classical codes. From nothing more than a split into trivial and nontrivial errors, we kinematically derive the MacWilliams transform as a Wigner-$D$ rotation between two canonical bases. Within each classical and quantum theory, changing the length $n$ leaves the rotation untouched: the same element simply reappears at spin $n/2$. And at fixed $n$, changing the rotation axis simply moves between the various classical and quantum theories.

</details>


### [112] [Reconfigurable Optical Platform for One-way Quantum Communication Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27181)
*Francesco Mazzoncini,Hugo Defienne,Romain Alléaume,Sylvain Gigan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了一种基于多模光纤和波前整形的可重构光学平台，用于实现单向量子通信复杂度问题，展示了指数级的量子-经典通信分离，并提供了实现更复杂量子优势协议的路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算优势的实用化演示仍是量子信息科学的核心目标，但量子计算霸权在技术上仍然具有挑战性。通信复杂度提供了一条在当前光子平台上展示量子优势的可行路径，因此需要开发相应的可重构硬件平台。

Method: 基于多模光纤和波前整形技术构建可重构光学平台，实现单向量子通信复杂度协议。通过实验验证了该平台能够实现已知的指数级量子-经典通信分离问题，并辅以数值模拟展示其在更广泛通信任务中的适用性。

Result: 实验成功实现了一个具有指数级量子-经典通信分离的单向量子通信复杂度问题。数值模拟表明该可重构解码架构可支持更一般的单向通信任务，且能在不增加硬件复杂度的情况下实现更高维度的扩展。

Conclusion: 多模光纤波前整形被确立为一种通用的单向量子通信复杂度硬件平台，为更苛刻的协议（其中更强的量子-经典分离可实现量子优势的实用演示）提供了具体的路线图。

Abstract: Demonstrating a practical quantum advantage remains a central goal in quantum information science. While quantum computational supremacy is still technologically demanding, communication complexity offers a promising route to showcase quantum advantage with current photonic platforms. Here we introduce a reconfigurable optical platform for one-way quantum communication complexity based on multimode fibers and wavefront shaping. We experimentally validate it by implementing a genuine one-way quantum communication complexity problem for which an exponential quantum--classical communication separation is known. Complementary numerical simulations show that the same reconfigurable decoding architecture can support more general one-way communication tasks with comparable performance, while also offering a route to higher-dimensional implementations without increasing hardware complexity. Together, these results establish multimode-fiber wavefront shaping as a versatile hardware platform for one-way quantum communication complexity and provide a concrete roadmap toward more demanding protocols, where stronger quantum--classical separations could enable practical demonstrations of quantum advantage.

</details>


### [113] [Robust quantum state certification and uncertainty principles for total influence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27184)
*Andrea Coladangelo,Jerry Li,Joseph Slote*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于几乎所有n量子比特目标态，仅使用非自适应单量子比特泡利测量即可实现最优的量子态检验，其样本复杂度达到信息论下界。技术核心是布尔函数总影响度的加权不确定性原理，延伸了超立方体上的海森堡不确定性原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在资源受限（仅单量子比特泡利测量）条件下，量子态检验能否达到信息论最优的样本复杂度，并解决此前未知的一般目标态检验问题。

Method: 1. 提出加权总影响度的不确定性原理，推广经典布尔函数分析中的Inf[f]与Inf[hat(f)]之和的下界。2. 将该原理与量子态检验问题建立联系，构造基于单量子比特泡利测量的检验协议。3. 通过信息论论证证明样本复杂度的最优性。

Result: 非自适应单量子比特泡利测量对于几乎所有（除2^{-Ω(n)}比例外）目标态，能够以O(ε^{-2}log(1/δ))的样本复杂度实现ε-接近与O(ε)-远离的区分，达到信息论下界。

Conclusion: 单量子比特泡利测量在几乎全体目标态上具有与联合测量相同的最优样本效率，揭示了量子态检验问题中测量能力与样本复杂度之间的深刻关系。

Abstract: We show that nonadaptive single-qubit Pauli measurements suffice to test whether an unknown $n$-qubit state $ρ$ is $\varepsilon$-close to or $O(\varepsilon)$-far from an ideal target state $|ψ\rangle$, for all but a $2^{-Ω(n)}$ fraction of target states. The test uses $O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/δ))$ copies of $ρ$ to achieve confidence $1-δ$, which is information-theoretically optimal even among protocols with arbitrary joint measurements.
  The main technical innovation is an uncertainty principle for weighted generalizations of the total influence of Boolean functions. As a simple example, the unweighted variant states that $\mathbf{Inf}[f]+\mathbf{Inf}[\widehat{f}] = Ω(n)$, which is a natural hypercube analogue of the Heisenberg uncertainty principle (here $\widehat{\,\cdot\,}$ denotes the $2^{-n/2}$-normalized Fourier transform). The weighted case generalizes $\mathbf{Inf}[\,\cdot\,]$ and $\mathbf{Inf}[\,\widehat{\,\cdot\,}\,]$ to Dirichlet energies associated with Glauber dynamics for certain dual measures on the cube.

</details>


### [114] [Sharp Bounds on Ground State Energy of the SYK Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27185)
*Arpon Basu,Pravesh K. Kothari,Siddhant Midha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了SYX哈密顿量的算子范数期望值约为√(2n)/k（对于超常数k≤o(√n)），通过识别一个确定性线性算子将问题转化为超图边空间上的扭曲玻色子模型，并利用Johnson方案的谱分析得到紧界。该结果扩展到了稀疏SYK模型，并证明了Basso等人的耗散量子算法可以高效计算SYK基态能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Garcia-Garcia等人2018年的预测并回答Feng等人2019年提出的开放问题，严格确定SYK哈密顿量算子范数的渐近行为。

Method: 1) 识别一个确定性线性算子x，使得x^{2ℓ}的二次型恰好等于SYK哈密顿量的期望迹矩；2) 将该算子视为超图边空间上的扭曲玻色子模型；3) 将问题约化为Johnson方案中一个自然(n choose k)维矩阵的谱分析；4) 通过构造一个具有大二次型的见证态来证明下界的紧性。

Result: 证明了E||H_SYK||_op = (1-o(1))·√(2n)/k 对于超常数k≤o(√n)成立，该结果扩展到了稀疏SYK模型，并作为推论证明了Basso等人的算法在k<√n/4时能以O(1)乘法因子计算基态能量。

Conclusion: 提供了SYK哈密顿量算子范数的严格紧界，建立了与Johnson方案谱理论的联系，并为相关量子算法的性能提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian $H_{\operatorname{SYK}}$ on $n$ Majorana modes with $k$-body interactions, and prove that $\mathbb{E}\|H_{\operatorname{SYK}}\|_{\operatorname{op}} = (1 - o(1))\cdot\sqrt{2n}/k$ for super-constant $k\leq o(\sqrt{n})$, where the expectation is over the disorder variables in the Hamiltonian. This confirms the predictions due to Garcia-Garcia, Jia and Verbaarschot'18 and answers a question posed in Feng, Tian and Wei'19. Our results extend to the sparse SYK Hamiltonian. As a corollary, we obtain that the dissipative quantum algorithm of Basso, Chen and Dalzell'24 provably computes the ground state energy of the SYK Hamiltonian up to an $O(1)$-multiplicative factor for all $k < \sqrt{n}/4$.
  Our key technical idea is identifying an explicit, deterministic linear operator $\mathsf{x}$ such that a fixed quadratic form of $\mathsf{x}^{2\ell}$ exactly equals the expected trace moments of the SYK Hamiltonian for every $n$ and $k$. This linear operator can be naturally viewed as a \emph{twisted} model of bosons on the space of hyperedges of a hypergraph. The problem thus reduces to identifying the spectral edge of $\mathsf{x}$, which we show is dominated by the spectrum of a natural ${n \choose k}$-dimensional matrix from the \emph{Johnson} scheme and is straightforward to compute using known results.
  To show that our bound is sharp, we construct a witness state with a large quadratic form on $\mathsf{x}$ and transform it into a certificate of a lower bound on the largest quadratic form on $H_{\operatorname{SYK}}$.

</details>


### [115] [Very Strong Irreversibility of Quantum Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27195)
*Tulja Varun Kondra,Raphael Brinster,Hermann Kampermann,Dagmar Bruß,Nikolai Wyderka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了量子纠缠操纵的不可逆性即使在指数级增长的误差下仍然存在，解决了Lami和Regula提出的猜想，并进一步推广到多项式增长误差的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠操纵中的不可逆性（即一些混合纠缠态需要纯纠缠来制备，但无法通过LOCC恢复出纯纠缠）是量子信息理论中的基本问题。已有研究表明这种不可逆性即使在最大类的不产生纠缠的操作下仍然存在，揭示了纠缠理论与热力学之间的根本区别。作者旨在证明这种不可逆性是极其顽固的——任何恢复可逆性的尝试都会导致随副本数指数增长的误差。

Method: 构造了指数强对偶可蒸馏纠缠与指数强对偶纠缠代价之间的严格分离；推导了非纠缠操作下指数强对偶代价的半定规划下界；针对完全PPT保持操作，构造了可解析求解的反对称态族。

Result: 证明了纠缠不可逆性即使在误差随副本数多项式增长时仍然存在（强于原猜想）；给出了指数强对偶代价的半定规划下界；构造了在完全PPT保持操作下表现出指数强对偶不可逆性的反对称态解析族。

Conclusion: 纠缠操纵的不可逆性是极其根本的，即使在允许指数级或多项式级误差的情况下也无法恢复可逆性。值得注意的是，即使在更受限的LOCC操作下，目前也还不知道类似的指数强对偶代价与可蒸馏纠缠之间的分离。

Abstract: The manipulation of quantum entanglement is fundamentally irreversible: some mixed entangled states require pure entanglement for their preparation, although no pure entanglement can be recovered from them by local operations and classical communication. This irreversibility is known to persist even under the maximal class of operations that do not generate entanglement, revealing a fundamental distinction between entanglement theory and thermodynamics. We construct cases for which any attempt to restore reversibility necessarily incurs an error that increases exponentially with the number of copies. Technically, we demonstrate a strict separation between the exponential strong-converse distillable entanglement and the exponential strong-converse entanglement cost. Our result resolves a conjecture posed by Lami and Regula (Nat. Phys. 19, 184-189 (2023)) and strengthens it by showing that the irreversibility of entanglement persists even at the level of polynomially (in the number of copies) growing error. We further derive a semidefinite-programming lower bound on the exponential strong-converse cost under non-entangling operations. Finally, for the class of completely PPT-preserving operations, we construct analytically solvable families of antisymmetric states exhibiting the exponential strong-converse irreversibility. Remarkably, to our knowledge, no analogous separation between exponential strong converse cost and the analogous distillable entanglement is currently known even under the more restrictive class of LOCC operations.

</details>


### [116] [Fault-Tolerant Logical Operations and Efficient State Preparation in Modular Quantum Architectures with Noisy Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27204)
*Siddardha Chelluri,Riccardo Mengoni,Tom Darras,Julien Laurat,Eleni Diamanti,Ioannis Lavdas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了模块化量子计算架构中分布式量子纠错的性能，通过电路级仿真分析了基于格点手术的非局域CNOT门在噪声环境下的逻辑错误率，发现接口能容忍比模块内高一个数量级的噪声，并提出了一种基于顶点覆盖问题的高效协议来制备分布式容错逻辑GHZ态，减少了辅助资源和开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越单片器件的资源限制，探索模块化量子计算中分布式量子纠错的实际可行性，量化接口噪声与模块内噪声对容错计算的影响差异。

Method: 采用旋转表面码对量子比特进行编码，通过电路级仿真实现基于格点手术的非局域CNOT门，并使用有噪贝尔对连接QPU；将辅助量子比特最小化问题建模为关联图上的顶点覆盖问题，提出多项式时间启发式算法。

Result: 接口可容忍的噪声比模块内噪声高一个数量级，且对容错阈值影响较小；提出的GHZ态制备协议显著降低了辅助开销、时间和非局域贝尔对消耗。

Conclusion: 分布式量子纠错能够支持模块化架构下可扩展的容错量子计算，为模块化量子计算的实用化提供了定量证据。

Abstract: Modular quantum computing is a leading paradigm for scaling quantum computation beyond the resource limitations of monolithic devices. In this architecture, multiple quantum processing units (QPUs), employing identical or distinct qubit modalities, are interconnected via shared entanglement. Here, we investigate how errors at module interfaces and within individual QPUs affect fault-tolerant computation when qubits are encoded using the rotated surface code. Going beyond the logical-memory benchmark, we perform circuit-level simulations of fault-tolerant nonlocal CNOT gates implemented via lattice surgery between QPUs connected by noisy Bell pairs, and analyze the resulting logical error rates. Our results show that interfaces can tolerate noise up to an order of magnitude higher than intra-QPU noise, with only a minor reduction in the fault-tolerance threshold. We further develop an efficient protocol for preparing distributed fault-tolerant logical GHZ states, reducing ancilla overhead, time, and nonlocal Bell-pair consumption. We show that ancilla minimization in this setting is equivalent to a vertex-cover problem on an associated graph, and introduce a polynomial-time heuristic algorithm for finding low-overhead solutions. Our results provide quantitative evidence that distributed quantum error correction can enable scalable, fault-tolerant quantum computation in modular architectures.

</details>


### [117] [Practical Quantum Topological Data Analysis with Applications to High-Dimensional Feature Extraction and Time Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27206)
*Jason Iaconis,Sayonee Ray,Samwel Sekwao,Claudio Girotto,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将量子拓扑数据分析（TDA）作为下游数据特征提取方法，通过提取组合拉普拉斯的低阶谱信息（如相对迹）作为高维拓扑的代理，并展示了在功能MRI和金融时间序列分析中的应用优势，同时给出了量子电路实现与资源估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典TDA计算高维拓扑特征成本高昂，现有量子TDA方法专注于精确或高精度Betti数估计，导致实用量子优势的窗口狭窄。作者希望将量子TDA重新定位为特征提取方法，以扩大其实用性。

Method: 提出基于矩的量子算法，提取组合拉普拉斯的低阶矩（包括相对迹）作为高维Betti信息的代理；在功能MRI和金融时间序列两个应用场景中验证高阶TDA特征对预测性能的提升；并在类似IonQ Tempo的钡基量子系统上进行实验验证。

Result: 低阶矩与高维Betti信息高度相关（即使相对Betti数很小）；高阶TDA特征在神经退行性疾病分类和金融市场不稳定识别中均提升了预测性能；给出了电路构造、资源估计、量子-经典交叉投影以及实验对比结果。

Conclusion: 量子TDA可作为从经典难以处理的数据中提取拓扑特征的实用方法，将量子TDA从精确Betti数估计转向低阶谱特征提取，拓宽了量子优势的实用场景。

Abstract: Topological data analysis (TDA) provides a powerful framework for extracting information about the shape of complex, unstructured data, but the classical cost of computing high dimensional topological features limits its application. Quantum algorithms for TDA offer a route around this bottleneck, yet existing approaches typically focus on exact or high precision Betti number estimation, making the regime for practical quantum advantage appear narrow. Here, we instead frame quantum TDA as a feature-extraction method for downstream data analysis by extracting low-order spectral information from the combinatorial Laplacian as a proxy for high-dimensional topology.
  We support this perspective from both the application and algorithmic sides. First, we show that higher-order TDA features improve predictive performance in two time-series applications: functional MRI analysis for neurodegenerative disease classification and financial time-series analysis for identifying market instability. Second, we develop a moment-based quantum algorithm and show that low-order moments, including the relative trace, are strongly correlated with high-dimensional Betti information, even when the relative Betti number is small. Finally, we present circuit constructions, resource estimates, quantum-classical crossover projections, and experimental results from a Barium development system similar to the forthcoming IonQ Tempo line, extracting Laplacian-derived observables from graph instances and quantitatively comparing them with exact Betti information.
  Together, these results establish quantum TDA as a practical approach for extracting topological features from classically challenging data

</details>
